      c,score_all,score_off = _local_conv(a)

   if not valid: basis=all, init=uniform

def _smooth_grad(x, eps, basis):
   a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
   n=a.size; L=_fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:M]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   if basis == 1: # off
       if n>1:
           m = c.copy(); m[n-1]=0.0
       else: m=np.zeros(M)
   else:
       m=c
   mx=float(m.max())
   if mx <= 1e-300:
       f=0.0 if basis==1 else 2.0*n*mx? For all mx=0 impossible if sum=1.
       gx=np.zeros_like(x)
       return f,gx
   # objective 2n*(mx + eps* logsumexp((m-mx)/eps))
   z=np.clip((m-mx)/eps, -745.0, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum())
   p=np.zeros(L); p[:M]=e/s
   f=2.0*n*(mx+eps*math.log(s))
   W=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
   ga=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(W)*np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]
   factor=2.0*n
   ga *= factor
   dot=np.dot(a,ga)
   gfull=a*(ga-dot)
   gx=gfull[:-1]
   if not finite... 
   return f,gx
```
Note: For off basis, `mx` central excluded. If all off c zero (onehot), mx=0, f=0, grad zero. L-BFGS no move. Good.

But is gradient formula factor correct? Let's test with simple case all, n=2, a normalized? a=[0.6,0.4], c=[0.36,0.48,0.16], all. p uniform? We can't test fully. The derivative of smooth F=2n∑p c with p normalized. The p depends on c, but for gradient we treat p as weights at current c (log-sum-exp gradient uses p including derivative of p? Actually F=mx+eps logsumexp has gradient p; derivative of p not needed because p is subgradient of that smooth function; derivative is exactly p times ∂c. Yes.
`ga` as gradient wrt a of ∑p c. We computed full g=2 ifft(conj(W)*conj(A)). Then multiply by 2n. Good.

def _local_block_search(a0,end,seed,basis):
   best=project(a0); best_off=official(best)

      val=official(cand)

```

Local block search details:
```
def _local_block_search(a0,end,seed,basis):
  a=project(a0); best=a.copy(); best_val=official(a)
  if not finite: best_val=local_score basis
  sigma=0.15
  while time<end:
    for _ in range(1+int(rng.integers(0,3))):
       w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(2), np.log(max(10,n//4)))))
       s = int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
       cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
    cand=project(cand)
    val=official(cand)
    if val<best_val-1e-12: accept, sigma=min(0.3, sigma*1.06)
    else: if no improve >3s: sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.8); if >15s and elapsed>30s: restart from best with tiny noise
  return best,best_val
```

Detailed helper implementations:

```
def _pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`_clean_nonnegative`:
```
def _clean_nonnegative(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(v,0.0)
```

`_project_simplex` as before. If v size 0 return.

`_ratios_from_a`: use last as reference. If all zeros, uniform. If last too small, set. Clip x [-45,45].

`_softmax_from_ratios`:
```
def _softmax_from_ratios(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    n=x.size+1
    if n==1: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.clip(x,-45,45)
    m=float(np.max(x)) if x.size else 0.0
    e=np.exp(np.clip(x-m,-745,709))
    last=np.exp(-m)
    s=e.sum()+last
    if ... uniform
    a=np.empty(n); a[:-1]=e/s; a[-1]=last/s; return a
```
Note if x max not finite after clip ok.

`_local_scores(a, L=None)`:
```
def _local_scores(a, L=None):
  v=_clean_nonnegative(a); n=v.size
  if n==0: return None, inf, inf
  s=v.sum()
  if s < 1e-12: return None, inf, inf
  v=v/s
  if L is None: L=_pow2(2*n-1)
  A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]
  c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
  c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  mx_all=float(c.max())
  M=2*n-1
  if n==1:
     mx_off=0.0
  else:
     co=c.copy(); co[n-1]=0.0; mx_off=float(co.max())
  score_all=2.0*n*mx_all
  score_off=2.0*n*mx_off
  return c,score_all,score_off
```
For n=1, M=1, center index 0; off empty. score_off=0.

`_official_score(seq)`:
```
def _official_score(seq):
    try:
       out=[float(x) for x in seq]
       # ensure finite? If any nonfinite return inf
       # evaluate_sequence also checks, but avoid errors
       for y in out:
          if not math.isfinite(y): return np.inf
       val=evaluate_sequence(out)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    try: val=float(val)
    except: return np.inf
    if not math.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return val
```
Use `math.isfinite` for float; val maybe np.float.

 cands=[]
 cands.append(('uniform', _uniform(n)))
 cands.append(('onehot0', _near_onehot(n,0,1e-8)))
 cands.append(('onehotmid', _near_onehot(n,n//2,1e-8))) # for off maybe
 if prev_a is not None: cands.append(('prev', _project_simplex(prev_a)))
 for p in [0.3,0.45,0.5,0.6,0.8,1.2]: ...
 for boost? maybe few
 random seeds 3: v=exp(rng.normal*0.5)*power; project
 return cands
```

```

`_power_sequence(n,p,offset=1.0)`:
```
j=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+offset

return _project_simplex(v)
```

`_random_sequence(n,seed)`:
```
rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
v=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,size=n))
v *= np.power(np.arange(n)+1, -0.5)
return _project_simplex(v)
```

def _official_parameter_search(base,start_time,end_time,seed):
   if _minimize is None: return base, inf? 
   n=base.size
   features=_make_features(n, 8)
   theta0=np.zeros(features.shape[1])
   maxfev=int(max(50, min(600, (end_time-start_time)*25))) # 25 calls/s
   # Use Nelder-Mead, options
   res=_minimize(_param_official, theta0, args=(base,features), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':3*maxfev, 'maxfev':maxfev, 'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-10, 'disp':0})
   theta=np.asarray(res.x)
   a=_build_param_candidate(theta,base,features)
   val=_official_score(a)
   if finite val: return a,val
   return base, inf
```

Local block search:
```
def _local_block_search(a0,end_time,seed,basis):
  a=_project_simplex(a0); best=a.copy()
  best_val=_official_score(best)
  _, best_local_all, best_local_off = _local_scores(best)
  best_local = best_local_off if basis==1 else best_local_all
  if not finite best_val: best_val = best_local
  if not finite best_local: best_local = best_val
  rng=...
  sigma=0.15
  last_improve=time.monotonic()
  start=last_improve
  while time<end_time:
     cand=best.copy(); nblocks=1+(int(rng.integers(0,2)) if rng else 0)
     maxw=max(5,n//8)
     for _ in range(nblocks):
        w=int(np.exp(rng.uniform(math.log(2.0), math.log(float(maxw)))))
        w=max(1,min(w,n))
        s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
        cand[s:s+w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma)))
     cand=_project_simplex(cand)
     _, loc_all, loc_off = _local_scores(cand)
     loc = loc_off if basis==1 else loc_all
     # If local clearly worse, skip official
     if finite(loc) and loc > best_local + 0.02 + 1e-9:
        # If basis all, central included, local all is actual; skip safe. For hidden, might skip official gain; but local guides.
        continue
     val=_official_score(cand)
     if finite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
        best=cand; best_val=val; best_local=loc; last_improve=now; sigma=min(0.3,sigma*1.05)
     else:
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now-last_improve > 4:
           sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.8)
           if now-last_improve > 15 and now-start>20:
              # perturb best with small noise
              cand=best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)); cand=project
              _,la,lo=_local_scores(cand); loc=...
              if finite(loc) and loc <= best_local + 0.05:
                  val=official(cand); if val<best_val: update
```
If official all and local filter skip, good. If hidden unrelated, local filter may skip many; but occasional noise restart evaluates. We can reduce skip tolerance over time: `skip_thresh = 1e-9 + 0.02*(1-frac)`. OK.

Need `rng.integers` returns numpy int; fine.

def _make_features(n, fdim):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,dtype=float) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    mats=[]
    mats.append(t)
    mats.append(t*t)
    mats.append(1.0-t)
    mats.append((1.0-t)**2)
    mats.append(np.exp(-5.0*t))
    mats.append(np.exp(-5.0*(1.0-t)))
    if n>1:
       mats.append(np.sin(np.pi*t))
    else: mats.append(np.zeros(n))
    if n>1:
       mats.append(np.cos(2.0*np.pi*t))
    else: ...
    present=min(fdim, len(mats))
    return np.column_stack(mats[:present])
```
If n=1, features columns constant; param search useless but okay.

Let's write `_run_minimize_once` carefully:
```
def _run_minimize_once(fun, x, args, maxiter, bounds):
    if _minimize is None:
        return x, 99
    if x.size==0: return x,1
    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'disp':0,'maxfun':int(max(100,10*maxiter)),'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12}
    try:
       res=_minimize(fun,x,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=options)
    except Exception:
       return x,99
    x_new=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    x_new=np.where(np.isfinite(x_new),x_new,0.0)
    x_new=np.clip(x_new,-45.0,45.0)
    status=0
    try: status=int(res.status)
    except: status=99
    return x_new,status
```
`maxfun` 10*maxiter; if maxiter50, 500. Good.

Could improve by using **multiplicative updates** instead of L-BFGS. For objective max c, a stationary point satisfies for active p, derivative d_i=2∑p_k (a_{k-i}+a_{k-i?}) constant. Multiplicative update: a_i ← a_i * exp(η (cont - d_i)), where cont is weighted average. This is simple and may be robust. We can implement:
```
Given a, p (softmax c/eps), compute d_i = gradient_weighted(p) / (2n) maybe derivative of weighted avg.
cont = ∑ a_i d_i / ∑a_i
a_new = a * exp(η (cont - d_i))
project sum=1
```
This is like Jensen-Shannon / projected subgradient. Can take many iterations, reasonably fast via FFT gradient. Could converge to flat derivative on active lags. We can combine with epsilon annealing.

def _exponentiated_gradient_search(a0, end_time, seed, tau0_frac=0.05, tau_min_frac=1e-4, max_iter=None):
  a=project(a0); best=a; best_score=local_all(a)
  n=a.size; L=pow2(2n-1); x=ratios(a)
  m,v? maybe simple.
  tau = tau0_frac * maxc
  steps_since_improve=0
  while time<end and (max_iter is None or iter<max_iter):
     c,score,_=local_scores(a)
     if score<best_score: best...
     mx=score/(2n)
     if mx<=0: break
     # weights p = exp((c-mx)/tau) / sum
     z=clip((c-mx)/tau, -700,0)
     e=exp(z); p=e/sum
     d = _weighted_gradient(a,p,L)  # derivative of sum p c = 2...
     # mean correction: const = sum(a*d) (since sum a=1)
     g = a*(d - const) # gradient wrt log-ratios (without L-BFGS)
     gnorm = sqrt(mean(g*g)) + 1e-15
     step_size adaptive: start 0.5 / gnorm? Actually g already small; we can set x_new = x - step * g / gnorm with step=1.0? Need scale.
```

Need `_weighted_gradient` top-level:
```
def _weighted_gradient(a,w,L):
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    g=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(W)*np.conj(A),n=L)[:a.size]
    return 2.0*g
```
But in exponentiated gradient, if we call `_local_scores` it already computes A and c. We can make `_local_scores_with_A` to return A. To avoid multiple FFTs, implement `_compute_conv(a,L)` returns c,A,score. Then gradient separate using A. Use in search.

```
def _convolve_normed(a,L):
   v=_clean... project? assume sum1
   A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]
   ...
   return c,A,score
```

Then `_grad_from_w(A,w,L,n)` uses A.

Now, low-dimensional parameter polish:
- Given base a, features matrix (n, d). Parameter theta. Build `a = _normalize(base * exp(features @ theta))` (simple divide by sum, not simplex projection because base positive). Use smooth objective `F = score_smooth(a, eps)`; gradient wrt theta can be computed:
  `ga = _score_grad_a(a, p)` (derivative of smooth score wrt a). For normalized log model log a = log base + Zθ - log S. da_i/dθ_k = a_i (Z_{ik} - zbar_k) where zbar_k=∑a_i Z_{ik}. Then gθ = ∑ a_i (Z_i - zbar)_k ga_i = Z^T (a*ga) - Z^T(a)*dot. We can compute `s = a*ga; gθ = features.T @ s - (features.T @ a)*dot`. Here dot=∑a_i ga_i. This is gradient wrt θ of F (no projection). Good.
- Use L-BFGS-B on theta bounds (-5,5) with smooth eps. This is low-dim and can improve global shape. We can call with `jac=True`.

def _upsample(a_small, n):
   if len= n return

Detailed EG update:
```
def _eg_search(a0,end,seed,initial_tau_frac=0.08,t min_frac=1e-4, max_iter=None):
  a=project(a0); x=ratios; best=a; best_score=score_all
  L=pow2(2n-1); rng
  lr=0.5
  # estimate maxc
  c,A,score = _conv_normed(a,L)
  maxc=score/(2n)
  tau=max(maxc*initial_tau_frac, 1e-300)
  no_impr=0
  while time<end and (max_iter is None or it<max_iter):
     # maybe compute c at start of loop from current a (if not from last)
     c,A,score = _conv_normed(a,L)
     maxc = score/(2n)
     if score<best_score-1e-12:
        best=a.copy(); best_score=score; last=now; no_impr=0; lr=min(1.5,lr*1.03)
     else:
        no_impr +=1
        if no_impr>100:
           tau=max(tau*0.7, maxc*tmin_frac)
           if no_impr>300:
              # reset to best with small noise
              a=project(best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))); x=ratios(a); lr=0.5
              no_impr=0
     # weights if maxc>0
     tau=max(maxc*tmin_frac, tau)
     z=(c-maxc)/tau; z=clip(z,-700,0); e=exp(z); s=e.sum(); p=e/s
     d=_grad_from_A(A,p,L,n)
     const=float(np.dot(a,d))
     g=a*(d-const)
     gnorm=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))+1e-300
     x_new=np.clip(x - lr*g/gnorm, -45,45)
     a_new=_softmax_from_ratios(x_new)
     # optional cache: evaluate score quickly? Next loop does; but we need know if accept to adapt lr. We can compute c_new, score_new using FFT (will also be used next). To avoid double, compute now.
     c_new,A_new,score_new=_conv_normed(a_new,L)
     if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
         a=a_new; x=x_new; lr=min(2.0, lr*1.08)
     else:
         # If score_new not too bad, accept for exploration with small probability? To avoid local? Maybe accept if less than a* (1+0.01)? But local exact; can use.
         if rng.random() < 0.02 and score_new < best_score*1.02:
             a=a_new; x=x_new; lr*=0.95
         else:
             lr=max(0.05, lr*0.85)
             # if very bad, revert
             # keep old a/x
     it+=1
     # adjust tau based on improvement? reduce slowly each 100 steps
     if it%50==0 and no_impr>20: tau=max(maxc*tmin_frac, tau*0.9)
```

def _local_block_search_local(a0,end,seed):
  a=project; best=a; best_score=local_all
  sigma=0.1; last_improve=now
  while time<end:
    cand=best.copy(); nblocks=1+int(rng.integers(0,3))
    for each block:
       w=int(np.exp(rng.uniform(log(2), log(max(10,n//4)))))
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
       cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
    cand=project(cand)
    sc=local_all(cand)
    if sc<best_score-1e-12: accept; sigma=min(0.35,sigma*1.05); last=now
    else: if no improve >3s: sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.8); if >15s restart tiny noise...

Let's outline final code:

```
"""
Search for nonnegative sequence minimizing 2n max(conv(a,a))/(sum a)^2.
Uses multi-scale exponentiated-gradient search, smoothed log-sum-exp L-BFGS,
low-dimensional parameter polishing, and multiplicative local search.
"""
import numpy as np, time, math
try: from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
except: _minimize=None

# helpers pow2, clean, project, ratios, softmax
# conv helpers
def _fft_len_for_n(n): return _pow2(2*n-1)
def _conv_state(a, L=None):
   v=_clean_nonnegative(a); n=v.size; s=v.sum()
   if n==0 or s < 1e-12: return None, None, n, inf
   v=v/s
   if L is None: L=_fft_len_for_n(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0); c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   mx=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   score=2.0*n*mx
   return v,A,c,score

def _score_all(a, L=None):
   v,A,c,sc=_conv_state(a,L); return sc
```
Need in search we often have a already normalized. `_conv_state` normalizes; overhead okay.

`_grad_weighted_from_A(A,w,L,n)`:
```
if n==0: empty
W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L)
g=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(W)*np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]
g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
return 2.0*g
```
Need w length L; we pass p length L.

def _two_power(n,p,off,tail)

```

`_eg_search` (core):
```
def _eg_search(a0, end_time, seed=0, max_iter=None, tau_frac0=0.25, tau_frac_min=1e-4, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    a=_project_simplex(a0); x=_ratios_from_a(a); n=a.size; L=...
    c,A,score=_conv_state(a,L)[2:] # returns v...
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=...
    lr=0.4
    tau_frac=tau_frac0
    last_improve=start
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time and (max_iter is None or it < max_iter):
        maxc = score/(2.0*n) if n else 0
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best=a.copy(); best_score=score; last_improve=now; no_impr=0; lr=min(1.2,lr*1.03)
        else:
            no_impr +=1
            if no_impr > 80:
                tau_frac=max(tau_frac_min, tau_frac*0.85)
            if no_impr > 350:
                # reset
                a=_project_simplex(best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.08,n)))
                x=_ratios_from_a(a)
                c,A,score=_conv_state(a,L); lr=0.4; no_impr=0; tau_frac=max(tau_frac0*0.5, tau_frac_min)
        if maxc <= 1e-300: break
        tau=max(maxc*tau_frac, 1e-300)
        z=np.clip((c-maxc)/tau, -700.0, 0.0)
        e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum())
        if not finite: p=np.full(2n-1,1/(2n-1)); pad
        p=np.zeros(L); p[:2*n-1]=e/s
        d=_grad_weighted_from_A(A,p,L,n)
        const=float(np.dot(a,d))
        g=a*(d-const)
        gnorm=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))+1e-300
        if gnorm < 1e-300: break
        x_new=np.clip(x - lr*g/gnorm, -45.0,45.0)
        a_new=_softmax_from_ratios(x_new)
        c_new,A_new,score_new=_conv_state(a_new,L)
        it +=1
        # accept if improves current or best or uphill
        accept = score_new < score - 1e-13
        if not accept and it % 100 == 0:
            if score_new < best_score * (1.0005) and rng.random() < 0.1:
                accept=True
        if accept:
            a=a_new; x=x_new; c=c_new; A=A_new; score=score_new
            lr=min(1.5, lr*1.02)
        else:
            lr=max(0.05, lr*0.9)
            # if very bad, do not update
    return best, best_score
```
Need in `_conv_state` returns 4 values. We should define `_, A, c, score = _conv_state(a, L)`.

Potential issue: If accept based only on current score, gradient may decrease weighted sum but increase max; it may reject often. But_best maybe updated when score<best. If reject, current unchanged; after many rejects, lr decreases, tau anneals. Good.

We can allow accept if score_new <= score + 1e-12 (flat). Use exact.

Implementation `_theta_smooth_grad(theta, base, features, eps)`:
```
th=np.asarray(theta); th=clip(-5,5)
lin=features.dot(th); clip(-20,20); a=base*np.exp(lin); a=_normalize(a)
n=len(a); L=...
A,c,score? Need smooth score f=2n*(cmax+eps logsumexp). Compute A,c.
p as smooth weights; factor=2n; d=_grad_weighted_from_A(A,p,L,n)
# gtheta = features.T @ (a*d) - dot(a,features.T@a)*d? Wait ga = factor*d? d is derivative of ∑p c. score_smooth factor 2n times. Let ga = factor*d.
s=a*ga
ga_sum=float(np.sum(s)) # dot(a,ga)
features_a = features.T @ a
g_theta = features.T @ s - ga_sum * features_a
# But derivative when normalized: log a = log base + Zθ - log S; da_i/dθ_k = a_i(Z_ik - (features_a)_k). First-order F: ∑ a_i ga_i (Z_ik - zbar_k) = (features.T@a_i ga_i)_k - gsum*features_a_k. Yes.

Let's think about EG performance. The update direction uses p over c length 2n-1, gradient d of weighted sum. But since p normalized to sum1, F_weighted =∑p c. Gradient d scale ~? We normalize g by gnorm and step lr. This is basically steepest descent in log-space with step size lr in RMS units. It might be slow if lr too small/large. We can adapt more intelligently: Use a line search? Maybe step size 0.3 normalized. If reject, reduce. For n=3809, many iterations. Could also use **Adam** on x using gradient (not normalized each time) to adapt per-coordinate. Previous `_projected_gradient_search` used Adam with weighted gradient mean-subtracted. Maybe better. Let's implement Adam-like EG:
- Compute gradient `g = a*(d-const)`.

```
def _refine_full(a0, end_time, seed):
    a=project; best_score=local
    total = end_time - now
    if total <= 0: return
    # 1. EG 25%
    end1 = now + 0.25*total
    a,sc = _eg_search(a,end1,seed, max_iter=10000)
    update
    # 2. L-BFGS smooth 35%
    end2 = now + 0.60*total
    if _minimize:
       a,sc = _lbb_smooth(a,end2,seed)
    # 3. theta 15%
    end3 = now + 0.75*total
    a,sc = _theta_polish(a,end3,seed)
    # 4. EG again 15%
    end4 = now + 0.90*total
    a,sc = _eg_search(a,end4,seed+123, max_iter=10000)
    # 5. local block 10%
    a,sc = _local_block_search(a,end_time,seed+456)
    return a,sc
```

`_power_objective_grad` maybe not used if we use q=2 in L-BFGS. Implement anyway.
```
def _power_objective_grad(x,q,basis): # basis ignore
  a=softmax; n; L; c; factor=2n
  f=factor*np.sum(c**q)
  w=np.zeros(L); w[:M]=c**(q-1)
  d=_grad_weighted_from_A(A,w,L,n)
  ga=factor*q*d
  dot=np.dot(a,ga); gx=a*(ga-dot)[:-1]
```
For q=2, if c small, gradient small. OK.

`_upsample` and `_downsample` using `np.interp`:
```
def _resample(a, n):
   v=clean; m=len(v); if m==n return project
   if m==0 return uniform
   pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,dtype=float)*m
   # subtract small half? Use positions from 0 to m-1.
   left=np.floor(pos); right=np.minimum(left+1,m-1); lefti=left.astype(int); righti=right.astype(int); frac=pos-left
   logv=np.log(np.maximum(v,1e-300))
   val=logv[lefti]*(1-frac)+logv[righti]*frac
   out=np.exp(np.clip(val,-700,700)); return project(out)
```

Detailed code snippets:

```
def _normalize(v):
    v = _clean_nonnegative(v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        n = v.size
        if n == 0: return v
        return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n))
    return v / s
```

Candidate scoring `_score_all`:
```
def _score_all(a, L=None):
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n==0: return inf
    if L is None: L = _pow2(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if mx < 0: mx=0
    return 2*n*mx
```
For search, to get c,A,score, define `_conv_normed` as above. It calls `_normalize` each time; if a already normalized, minor.

Could optimize by not reclean in `_conv_normed` if known, but okay.

Code for `_resample`:
```
def _resample(a, n):
    v=_clean_nonnegative(a).ravel()
    m=v.size
    if n<=0: return v
    if m==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    if m==n: return _normalize(v)
    if m==1:
        out=np.full(n, v[0])
        return _normalize(out)
    pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,dtype=float)*m
    pos=np.clip(pos,0.0,float(m-1))
    fv=np.log(np.maximum(v,1e-300))
    val=np.interp(pos, np.arange(m,dtype=float), fv)
    out=np.exp(np.clip(val,-700,700))
    return _normalize(out)
```
`np.interp` top-level.

`_eg_search` details with current c/A/score:
```
def _eg_search(a0, end_time, seed=0, max_iter=None, tau_frac0=0.25, tau_frac_min=1e-4):
    a=_normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a,np.inf
    x=_ratios_from_a(a)
    L=_pow2(2*n-1)
    _,A,c,score=_conv_state(a,L)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    lr=0.4
    tau_frac=tau_frac0
    no_impr=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time and (max_iter is None or it < max_iter):
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       maxc=score/(2.0*n) if n else 0
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_impr=0; lr=min(1.5, lr*1.04)
       else:
          no_impr += 1
          if no_impr > 80:
             tau_frac=max(tau_frac_min, tau_frac*0.85)
          if no_impr > 400:
             base = best + 1e-12
             noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.08,size=n))
             a = _normalize(base*noise)
             x = _ratios_from_a(a)
             _,A,c,score=_conv_state(a,L)
             lr=0.4
             no_impr=0
             tau_frac=max(tau_frac0*0.5, tau_frac_min)
             it += 1
             continue
       if maxc <= 1e-300:
          break
       tau=max(maxc*tau_frac, 1e-300)
       M=2*n-1
       z=(c-maxc)/tau
       z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
       e=np.exp(z)
       s=float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
          p=np.zeros(L); p[:M]=1.0/float(M)
       else:
          p=np.zeros(L); p[:M]=e/s
       d=_grad_weighted_from_A(A,p,L,n)
       const=float(np.dot(a,d))
       g=a*(d-const)
       gnorm=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))
       if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm <= 1e-300:
          break
       x_new=np.clip(x - lr * (g/gnorm), -45.0,45.0)
       a_new=_softmax_from_ratios(x_new)
       _,A_new,c_new,score_new=_conv_state(a_new,L)
       it += 1
       accept = np.isfinite(score_new) and score_new < score - 1e-13
       if not accept:
          # occasional acceptance to escape: if not much worse
          if score_new < best_score * 1.002 and rng.random() < 0.03:
             accept=True
       if accept:
          a=a_new; x=x_new; A=A_new; c=c_new; score=score_new
          lr=min(2.0, lr*1.02)
       else:
          lr=max(0.05, lr*0.9)
    return best, best_score
```
If recenter uses `base=best + 1e-12` but if best has large values? best sum1, okay.

def _local_block_search(a0,end_time,seed):
 a=normalize; best...; rng; sigma=0.12; last_improve=now; start=now; best_score=score
 while time<end:
  cand=best.copy(); nblocks=1+int(rng.integers(0,2))
  maxw=max(5,n//8)
  for: w int exp(log(2),log(maxw)); s; cand[s:s+w]*=exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
  cand=normalize(cand)
  sc=score_all(cand)
  if sc<best_score-1e-12: accept; sigma=min(0.35,sigma*1.06); last=now
  else:
    if now-last>3: sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.85)
    if now-last>15 and now-start>30:
       cand=(best+1e-12)*exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,n)); normalize; sc; if sc<best: update
 return best,best_score
```

def _lbb_smooth(a0,end_time,seed):

 eps_rel=0.1; no=0
 while time<end_time:

 return best,best_score

Top-level:
```
def _smooth_a_grad(a, eps):
   v=_normalize(a); n=v.size
   if n==0: return 1e300, zeros
   L=_pow2(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0); c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   cmax=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   if cmax <= 1e-300:
       return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
   eps=max(float(eps),1e-300)
   z=np.clip((c-cmax)/eps,-700,0)
   e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum())
   p=np.zeros(L); p[:2*n-1]=e/s if s>0 else 1/(2n-1)
   f=2.0*n*(cmax+eps*math.log(s) if s>0 else cmax)
   d=_grad_weighted_from_A(A,p,L,n)
   ga=2.0*n*d
   return f,ga
```
If s underflow? c-cmax <=0, at least max e=1, s≥1. So log ok.

return f,ga

def _smooth_objective_grad(x, eps):
    a=_softmax_from_ratios(x)
    f,ga=_smooth_a_grad(a,eps)
    if not finite f: return 1e300, zeros
    dot=np.dot(a,ga)
    gx=a*(ga-dot); gx=gx[:-1]
    if not finite gx: gx=zeros
    return f,gx
```

Feature gradient:
```
def _theta_smooth_grad(theta, base, features, eps):
   a=_build_from_theta(theta,base,features)
   f,ga=_smooth_a_grad(a,eps)
   if not finite f: return 1e300, zeros len(theta)
   s=a*ga
   fsum=float(np.sum(s))
   fa=features.T @ a
   g=features.T @ s - fsum*fa
   if not finite: g=zeros
   return f,g
```

```

def _smooth_descend(a0, end_time, seed=0, max_iter=None, eps_frac0=0.25, eps_frac_min=1e-4):
   a=_normalize(a0); x=_ratios_from_a(a); n=a.size
   score=_score_all(a); best=a; best_score=score
   rng=...; lr=0.35; eps_frac=eps_frac0; no=0; it=0
   while time<end and it<max_iter:
      if not finite score: break
      maxc=score/(2n)
      eps=max(maxc*eps_frac,1e-12)

      if not finite g or allclose? gnorm=...

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2

The objective is scale invariant, so the search is done on the positive
simplex.  The code uses:

  * multi-scale initialization (optimizing shorter lengths and resampling),
  * exact FFT-based local scoring,
  * annealed projected gradient on a smooth log-sum-exponential surrogate,
  * L-BFGS polishing on q-norm and smooth-max surrogates,
  * low-dimensional feature-space polishing,
  * multiplicative block local search,
  * optional final trial at double length if time permits.

The returned sequence is the best candidate according to the official
evaluate_sequence() when it is available, with local FFT scoring as a
fallback/estimator.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
except Exception:
    _minimize = None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_nonnegative(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(v, 0.0)


def _normalize(v):
    v = _clean_nonnegative(v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        n = v.size
        if n == 0:
            return v.copy()
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def _ratios_from_a(a):
    """
    Log-ratio parameterization:
        x_i = log(a_i / a_last), i = 0..n-2
    """
    a = _clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s

    if a[-1] <= 1e-200:
        a[-1] = 1e-8
        a = a / float(a.sum())

    ref = max(float(a[-1]), 1e-300)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a[:-1], 1e-300) / ref)
    return np.clip(x, -45.0, 45.0).astype(np.float64)


def _softmax_from_ratios(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -45.0, 45.0)
    n = x.size + 1
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(x)) if x.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(np.clip(x - m, -745.0, 700.0))
    last = float(np.exp(-m))
    s = float(e.sum()) + last

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[:-1] = e / s
    out[-1] = last / s
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Convolution / objective helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fft_len_for_n(n):
    return _pow2(2 * int(max(1, n)) - 1)


def _conv_state(a, L=None):
    """
    Returns normalized vector, its rfft, linear circular-convolved with itself
    truncated to length 2n-1, and the exact local score.
    """
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v, None, None, np.inf

    if L is None:
        L = _fft_len_for_n(n)

    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0

    score = 2.0 * float(n) * mx
    return v, A, c, score


def _score_all(a, L=None):
    _, _, _, sc = _conv_state(a, L)
    return sc


def _grad_weighted_from_A(A, w, L, n):
    """
    Gradient wrt a (length n) of sum_k w_k (a*a)_k,
    where w is padded to length L.
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    g = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(W) * np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * g


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Smooth surrogates and their gradients
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _smooth_a_grad(a, eps):
    """
    Smooth max surrogate:
        2n*(max(c) + eps*logsumexp((c-max(c))/eps))
    Returns (value, gradient wrt a) on the normalized simple.
    """
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = _fft_len_for_n(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    eps = float(max(eps, 1e-300))
    z = (c - cmax) / eps
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())

    p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        p[:2 * n - 1] = e / s
        val = 2.0 * float(n) * (cmax + eps * math.log(s))
    else:
        p[:2 * n - 1] = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
        val = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    d = _grad_weighted_from_A(A, p, L, n)
    ga = 2.0 * float(n) * d
    ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
    return float(val), ga


def _power_a_grad(a, q):
    """
    Objective 2n * sum(c_k^q).
    Returns (value, gradient wrt a).
    """
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = _fft_len_for_n(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    q = float(max(q, 1.0000001))
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.sum(np.power(c, q)))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[:2 * n - 1] = np.power(c, q - 1.0)
    d = _grad_weighted_from_A(A, w, L, n)
    ga = 2.0 * float(n) * q * d
    ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
    return val, ga


def _smooth_objective_grad(x, eps):
    a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
    f, ga = _smooth_a_grad(a, eps)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e300

    if a.size == 0:
        return f, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gx = a * (ga - dot)
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    if x.size > 0:
        gx = gx[:-1]
    else:
        gx = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return f, gx


def _power_objective_grad(x, q):
    a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
    f, ga = _power_a_grad(a, q)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e300

    if a.size == 0:
        return f, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gx = a * (ga - dot)
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    if x.size > 0:
        gx = gx[:-1]
    else:
        gx = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return f, gx


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Optimizer wrappers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _run_minimize_once(fun, x, args, maxiter, bounds):
    if _minimize is None:
        return np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 99

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x, 1

    if bounds is None:
        bounds = [(-45.0, 45.0)] * int(x.size)

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(max(100, 12 * int(maxiter))),
        "ftol": 1e-18,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "disp": 0,
    }

    try:
        res = _minimize(
            fun,
            x,
            args=args,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception:
        return x, 99

    x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.where(np.isfinite(x_new), x_new, 0.0)
    x_new = np.clip(x_new, -45.0, 45.0)

    status = 0
    try:
        status = int(res.status)
    except Exception:
        status = 99

    return x_new, status


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Search loops
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _smooth_descend(a0, end_time, seed=0, max_iter=None, eps_frac0=0.25, eps_frac_min=1e-4):
    """
    Projected gradient descent on the smooth max surrogate, with annealing
    and restarts.  Acceptance is by exact local score.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    x = _ratios_from_a(a)
    score = _score_all(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return a, np.inf

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    lr = 0.35
    eps_frac = float(eps_frac0)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time and (max_iter is None or it < max_iter):
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        maxc = float(score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        eps = max(maxc * eps_frac, 1e-12)

        try:
            _, g = _smooth_objective_grad(x, eps)
        except Exception:
            g = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g))) if g.size else 0.0

        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm <= 1e-300:
            break

        noise_amp = 0.0
        if no_improve > 250:
            noise_amp = 0.02

        step = lr * (g / gnorm)
        if noise_amp > 0.0:
            step = step + rng.normal(0.0, noise_amp, size=max(0, x.size))

        x_new = np.clip(x - step, -45.0, 45.0)
        a_new = _softmax_from_ratios(x_new)
        score_new = _score_all(a_new)
        it += 1

        accept = np.isfinite(score_new) and score_new < score - 1e-13
        if not accept:
            if np.isfinite(score_new) and score_new < best_score * 1.002 and rng.random() < 0.02:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a = a_new
            x = x_new
            score = float(score_new)

            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = score
                no_improve = 0
                lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.03)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            lr = max(0.05, lr * 0.90)
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 70:
            eps_frac = max(eps_frac_min, eps_frac * 0.85)

        if no_improve > 350:
            base = best + 1e-12
            base = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, size=n))
            a = _normalize(base)
            x = _ratios_from_a(a)
            score = _score_all(a)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                score = best_score
                a = best.copy()
                x = _ratios_from_a(a)
            lr = 0.35
            eps_frac = max(eps_frac0 * 0.5, eps_frac_min)
            no_improve = 0

    return best, best_score


def _lbb_smooth(a0, end_time, seed=0):
    """
    L-BFGS polishing on q=2 sum-of-squares, then annealed smooth max.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or _minimize is None:
        return a, _score_all(a)

    score = _score_all(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    x = _ratios_from_a(a)
    bounds = [(-45.0, 45.0)] * int(x.size)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1e-6, float(end_time - start))
    end_q2 = start + 0.25 * total

    while time.monotonic() < end_q2:
        x, status = _run_minimize_once(_power_objective_grad, x, (2.0,), 35, bounds)
        if status == 1:
            break
        a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
        sc = _score_all(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)

    if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
        return best, best_score

    eps_frac = 0.10
    no_improve = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        maxc = max(best_score / (2.0 * float(n)), 1e-14)
        eps = max(maxc * eps_frac, 1e-12)

        x, status = _run_minimize_once(_smooth_objective_grad, x, (eps,), 50, bounds)
        a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
        sc = _score_all(a)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            no_improve = 0
            eps_frac = max(0.02, eps_frac * 0.75)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=max(0, x.size)), -45.0, 45.0)
            eps_frac = max(0.01, eps_frac * 0.70)

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional feature polishing
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _make_features(n, fdim=10):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty((0, fdim), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    mats = [
        t,
        t * t,
        (1.0 - t),
        (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t),
        np.exp(-5.0 * t),
        np.exp(-12.0 * t),
        np.exp(-5.0 * (1.0 - t)),
        np.exp(-12.0 * (1.0 - t)),
        np.sin(math.pi * t),
        np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * t),
    ]
    fdim = min(int(fdim), len(mats))
    return np.column_stack(mats[:fdim]).astype(np.float64)


def _build_from_theta(theta, base, features):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)

    lin = features.dot(theta)
    lin = np.where(np.isfinite(lin), lin, 0.0)
    lin = np.clip(lin, -20.0, 20.0)

    a = base * np.exp(lin)
    return _normalize(a)


def _theta_smooth_grad(theta, base, features, eps):
    a = _build_from_theta(theta, base, features)
    f, ga = _smooth_a_grad(a, eps)
    dth = theta.size
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(dth, dtype=np.float64)

    s = a * ga
    gsum = float(np.sum(s))
    fa = features.T @ a
    g = features.T @ s - gsum * fa
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(f), g


def _theta_local_score(theta, base, features):
    a = _build_from_theta(theta, base, features)
    sc = _score_all(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e300
    return float(sc)


def _theta_polish(a0, end_time, seed=0):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or _minimize is None:
        return a, _score_all(a)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(_score_all(best))
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    features = _make_features(n, 10)
    fdim = features.shape[1]

    # Exact Nelder-Mead in low-dimensional multiplicative-feature space.
    exact_end = min(end_time, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.12 * (end_time - time.monotonic())))
    if time.monotonic() < exact_end:
        theta = np.zeros(fdim, dtype=np.float64)
        maxfev = int(max(80, min(500, 30.0 * max(0.1, exact_end - time.monotonic()))))
        try:
            res = _minimize(
                _theta_local_score,
                theta,
                args=(best, features),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={
                    "maxfev": maxfev,
                    "xatol": 1e-5,
                    "fatol": 1e-10,
                    "disp": 0,
                    "maxiter": 3 * maxfev,
                },
            )
            theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            a2 = _build_from_theta(theta, best, features)
            sc = _score_all(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = a2
                best_score = float(sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Smooth L-BFGS polishing in the same low-dimensional space.
    eps_frac = 0.06
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 4242)
    bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * fdim

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        base = _normalize(best)
        theta = np.zeros(fdim, dtype=np.float64)
        maxc = max(best_score / (2.0 * float(n)), 1e-14)
        eps = max(maxc * eps_frac, 1e-12)

        try:
            res = _minimize(
                _theta_smooth_grad,
                theta,
                args=(base, features, eps),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 80,
                    "maxfun": 300,
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "disp": 0,
                },
            )
            theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
        except Exception:
            theta = np.zeros(fdim, dtype=np.float64)

        a2 = _build_from_theta(theta, base, features)
        sc = _score_all(a2)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a2
            best_score = float(sc)
            eps_frac = max(0.01, eps_frac * 0.75)
        else:
            # If low-dimensional polish stalls, stop; the full-space stages continue.
            break

    return best, best_score


def _local_block_search(a0, end_time, seed=0):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    score = _score_all(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sigma = 0.12
    last_improve = time.monotonic()
    start = last_improve

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        cand = best.copy()
        nblocks = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 2))
        maxw = max(5, n // 8)

        for _ in range(nblocks):
            w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(math.log(2.0), math.log(float(maxw)))))
            w = max(1, min(w, n))
            s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
            cand[s:s + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))

        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_all(cand)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = float(sc)
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            sigma = min(0.35, sigma * 1.06)
        else:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_improve > 3.0:
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.85)
            if now - last_improve > 15.0 and now - start > 30.0:
                base = best + 1e-12
                base = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
                cand = _normalize(base)
                sc = _score_all(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Resampling and candidate generation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _resample(a, n):
    v = _clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    m = v.size
    n = int(max(1, n))

    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return _normalize(v)

    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(v[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64) * float(m)
    pos = np.clip(pos, 0.0, float(m - 1))
    logv = np.log(np.maximum(v, 1e-300))
    val = np.interp(pos, np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64), logv)
    out = np.exp(np.clip(val, -700.0, 700.0))
    return _normalize(out)


def _power_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + float(offset)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
    v = np.power(x, -float(p))
    return _normalize(v)


def _two_tail_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0, tail_weight=0.02):
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = np.power(np.maximum(j + float(offset), 1e-8), -float(p))
    tail = np.power(np.maximum((n - 1.0 - j) + float(offset), 1e-8), -float(p))
    v = base + float(tail_weight) * tail
    return _normalize(v)


def _random_sequence(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    v = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n))
    v *= np.power(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0, -0.5)
    return _normalize(v)


def _load_previous(n_max=20000):
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None

    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size < 16 or a.size > int(n_max):
        return None
    return _normalize(a)


def _score_candidates(cands):
    scored = []
    for idx, (name, a) in enumerate(cands):
        a = _normalize(a)
        sc = _score_all(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), idx, name, a))
    scored.sort()
    return scored


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Refinement of one start
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _refine_start(a0, end_time, seed=0):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    sc = _score_all(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return a, np.inf

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(sc)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1e-6, float(end_time - start))

    # 1. Smooth projected gradient with annealing.
    end1 = start + 0.28 * total
    a_try, sc_try = _smooth_descend(best, end1, seed=seed + 17,
                                   eps_frac0=0.30, eps_frac_min=1e-4)
    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-13:
        best = a_try
        best_score = float(sc_try)

    # 2. L-BFGS polishing.
    end2 = start + 0.62 * total
    if time.monotonic() < end2 and _minimize is not None:
        a_try, sc_try = _lbb_smooth(best, end2, seed=seed + 31)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a_try
            best_score = float(sc_try)

    # 3. Low-dimensional feature polish.
    end3 = start + 0.78 * total
    if time.monotonic() < end3 and _minimize is not None:
        a_try, sc_try = _theta_polish(best, end3, seed=seed + 53)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a_try
            best_score = float(sc_try)

    # 4. Additional smooth projected gradient.
    end4 = start + 0.92 * total
    if time.monotonic() < end4:
        a_try, sc_try = _smooth_descend(best, end4, seed=seed + 79,
                                       eps_frac0=0.18, eps_frac_min=1e-5)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a_try
            best_score = float(sc_try)

    # 5. Exact-block local polish.
    if time.monotonic() < end_time:
        a_try, sc_try = _local_block_search(best, end_time, seed=seed + 101)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = a_try
            best_score = float(sc_try)

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Official evaluator wrapper
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _official_score(seq):
    try:
        out = [float(x) for x in seq]
        val = evaluate_sequence(out)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2390, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2390.0
    budget = max(30.0, min(2390.0, budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 6.0

    # Choose a construction length.
    prev = _load_previous(20000)
    n = 3809
    if prev is not None and 64 <= prev.size <= 8000:
        n = int(prev.size)

    # Basic candidate set.
    cands = []
    cands.append(("uniform", np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)))
    if prev is not None:
        cands.append(("prev", _normalize(prev)))

    powers = [
        (0.45, 0.5), (0.45, 1.0), (0.45, 1.5),
        (0.475, 0.5), (0.475, 1.0), (0.475, 1.5),
        (0.48, 0.25), (0.48, 0.5), (0.48, 1.0), (0.48, 1.5),
        (0.50, 0.25), (0.50, 0.5), (0.50, 1.0), (0.50, 1.5),
        (0.52, 0.5), (0.52, 1.0), (0.52, 1.5),
        (0.55, 0.5), (0.55, 1.0),
        (0.60, 0.5), (0.60, 1.0),
    ]
    for p, off in powers:
        cands.append((f"power_{p}_{off}", _power_sequence(n, p, off)))

    for tw in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08):
        for p in (0.48, 0.50):
            cands.append((f"tw_{p}_{tw}", _two_tail_sequence(n, p, 1.0, tw)))

    for s in (101, 202, 303, 404):
        cands.append(("rand", _random_sequence(n, seed + s)))

    # Initial local scores.
    scored = _score_candidates(cands)
    if not scored:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        out = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            if not math.isfinite(_official_score(out)):
                out = [float(x) for x in _power_sequence(n, 0.5, 1.0)]
        except Exception:
            pass
        return out

    best_a = np.asarray(scored[0][3], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_sc = float(scored[0][0])

    print(f"length={n} initial_best_local={best_sc:.10f}")

    # If enough time, build multi-scale candidates from short optimized lengths.
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 180.0:
        top_short_starts = []
        for i in range(min(3, len(scored))):
            top_short_starts.append(scored[i][3])
        if prev is not None:
            top_short_starts.append(_normalize(prev))

        for m in (64, 128, 256):
            small_starts = []
            for s in top_short_starts:
                small_starts.append(_resample(s, m))
            small_starts.append(_power_sequence(m, 0.48, 0.8))
            small_starts.append(_power_sequence(m, 0.50, 1.0))
            small_starts.append(_random_sequence(m, seed + m))

            for si, sstart in enumerate(small_starts[:6]):
                short_try, sc_short = _smooth_descend(
                    sstart,
                    min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 2.5),
                    seed=seed + 700 + 10 * m + si,
                    max_iter=1800,
                    eps_frac0=0.30,
                    eps_frac_min=1e-3,
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc_short):
                    cand = _resample(short_try, n)
                    sc = _score_all(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        cands.append((f"small_{m}_{si}", cand))
                        scored2 = _score_candidates([(f"small_{m}_{si}", cand)])
                        # keep the master list coarse but add the candidate
                        # to the merged scored list by rescoring it above.

        cands.append(("uniform", np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)))
        scored = _score_candidates(cands)
        if scored:
            if scored[0][0] < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_a = np.asarray(scored[0][3], dtype=np.float64).copy()
                best_sc = float(scored[0][0])
                print(f"after_small local={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Refine the best few initial / multi-scale starts.
    top = [(sc, idx, name, a) for (sc, idx, name, a) in scored if np.isfinite(sc)][:6]
    if not top:
        top = [(best_sc, 0, "initial", best_a)]

    fractions = [0.42, 0.26, 0.15, 0.10, 0.06, 0.05]
    total_budget = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    for i,(sc0, _, _, a0) in enumerate(top):
        if i >= len(fractions):
            break
        now = time.monotonic()
        stage_budget = fractions[i] * total_budget
        if stage_budget < 5.0:
            break
        end_stage = min(deadline, now + stage_budget)

        try:
            a_try, sc_try = _refine_start(a0, end_stage, seed=seed + 1000 + 37 * i)
        except Exception:
            a_try, sc_try = a0, _score_all(a0)

        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_a = np.asarray(a_try, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_sc = float(sc_try)
            print(f"start_{i} local={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Optional: try doubling the length if enough time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 420.0 and 100 <= best_a.size <= 5000:
        n2 = 2 * int(best_a.size)
        if n2 <= 12000:
            long_start = _resample(best_a, n2)
            long_end = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 260.0)
            long_a, long_sc = _smooth_descend(
                long_start,
                long_end,
                seed=seed + 7777,
                eps_frac0=0.22,
                eps_frac_min=2e-5,
            )
            if np.isfinite(long_sc):
                long_a, long_sc = _local_block_search(long_a, min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0), seed + 8888)

            if np.isfinite(long_sc) and long_sc < best_sc - 1e-8:
                best_a = long_a
                best_sc = float(long_sc)
                print(f"longer_length local={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Final candidate set and official-evaluator selection.
    final_cands = [best_a]
    if prev is not None:
        final_cands.append(_normalize(prev))
    final_cands.append(np.full(best_a.size if best_a.size else n, 1.0 / float(max(1, best_a.size if best_a.size else n)), dtype=np.float64))

    best_final = None
    best_final_key = (np.inf, np.inf)

    for cand in final_cands:
        cand = _normalize(cand)
        off = _official_score(cand)
        loc = _score_all(cand)

        if math.isfinite(off):
            key = (float(off), float(loc) if math.isfinite(loc) else 1e300)
        else:
            key = (np.inf, float(loc) if math.isfinite(loc) else 1e300)

        if key < best_final_key:
            best_final = cand
            best_final_key = key

    if best_final is None:
        best_final = best_a

    best_final = _normalize(best_final)
    out = [float(x) for x in best_final]

    try:
        val = _official_score(out)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            # Fallback to a known-valid simple candidate.
            out = [float(x) for x in _power_sequence(len(out), 0.5, 1.0)]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
